Proof 1: Kepler's Third Law: T^2 proportional to a^3

Statement: Kepler's Third Law: T^2 proportional to a^3

Hypotheses: two-body system, central force

Conclusion: T^2 = (4*pi^2/G*M) * a^3

Definitions: orbital period, semi-major axis, gravitational

Proof (Variation #1):

Step 1: Circular orbit: centripetal = gravitational

Step 2: mv^2/r = GMm/r^2

Step 3: v = 2*pi*r/T

Step 4: Substitute and solve: T^2 = 4*pi^2*r^3/(GM)

ID: a808336d8ae7 | Status: UNFORMALIZED | Complexity: O(n)

Mutation test: Fails for relativistic orbits or perturbed systems

Provenance: Variation #1 for theorem in domain Astronomy

Hypotheses: two-body system, central force

Conclusion: T^2 = (4*pi^2/G*M) * a^3

Definitions: orbital period, semi-major axis, gravitational

Step 2: mv^2/r = GMm/r^2

Step 3: v = 2*pi*r/T

Step 4: Substitute and solve: T^2 = 4*pi^2*r^3/(GM)
